TL;DR: 本文通过结合量子拒绝采样（QRS）与Klein算法的下界分析，实现了截断对偶q-ary格高斯采样的二次量子加速，并将其应用于对偶攻击和GPV签名方案，显著降低了攻击成本估计（对Kyber系列分别降低9、4、13比特）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对偶攻击和GPV陷门采样中的格高斯采样步骤是整体复杂度的主要瓶颈，现有经典采样方法效率不足，需要量子加速来降低攻击成本。

Method: 将Wang和Ling分析Klein算法所得的下界（给出逐点支配条件）与Ozols等人提出的量子拒绝采样（QRS）框架相结合，在给定截断Klein提议分布的相干预言机访问下，通过QRS实现对截断对偶q-ary格高斯的量子制备，获得采样复杂度的二次缩减。截断半径选择使截断分布与完整格高斯的全变差距离可忽略。

Result: 将对偶攻击中的经典采样器替换为该量子采样器后，在相同参数下，对Kyber-512、Kyber-768、Kyber-1024的攻击成本分别降低9、4、13比特；同时给出了模切换下的对应估计。在GPV签名中，用QRS替代MCMC采样器，同样实现了二次加速。

Conclusion: 量子拒绝采样可有效加速格高斯采样这一瓶颈步骤，从而提升对偶攻击和GPV签名的量子攻击效率，对后量子密码标准化参数的安全评估具有重要影响。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the dual attack and GPV trapdoor sampling, focusing on the lattice Gaussian sampling term, which can be a significant bottleneck in the overall complexity. We show that this sampling step can be quantumly accelerated by combining the lower bound underlying Wang and Ling's analysis of Klein's algorithm with the quantum rejection sampling (QRS) framework proposed by Ozols et al. Specifically, this lower bound gives precisely the pointwise domination condition required for quantum rejection sampling when given coherent oracle access to a truncated Klein proposal distribution, which yields a quantum procedure for preparing the truncated dual $q$-ary lattice Gaussian with a quadratic reduction in the sampling complexity. The truncation radius is chosen so that the truncated distribution is negligibly close to the full lattice Gaussian in total variation distance. Substituting this sampler into the dual attack framework results in reduced overall attack-cost estimates. Compared with Pouly and Shen's modern dual attack under the same parameter choices, our estimates reduce the attack cost by \(9\), \(4\), and \(13\) bits for Kyber-512, Kyber-768, and Kyber-1024, respectively. We also report the corresponding estimates with modulus switching. Finally, by replacing the Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampler with the QRS algorithm, we achieve a similar quadratic speedup in the GPV signing process.

</details>


### [94] [Benchmark for Quantum Teleportation with Non-Uniform Prior Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24861)
*Tomáš Opatrný,Allison Brattley,Kunal K. Das*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非均匀先验分布（von Mises-Fisher分布）下的量子隐形传态，利用贝叶斯方法推导了不使用纠缠时的最优平均保真度基准，证明其可显著高于均匀分布假设下的阈值，并推广到多量子比特自旋相干态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子隐形传态基准仅适用于均匀分布输入，当输入状态具有非均匀先验分布（存在先验知识）时，原有基准不再适用，亟需建立新的基准函数以正确判定量子优势。

Method: 采用贝叶斯方法，针对von Mises-Fisher分布的量子态，推导单量子比特投影测量在特定布洛赫轴上的最优基准保真度；同时利用基于外差探测的相干自旋POVM实现类似结果；并将方法推广至N体自旋相干态系统（N∈[1,∞)）。

Result: 非均匀分布下的保真度阈值显著高于均匀分布假设下的传统基准；建立了涵盖任意粒子数N的自旋相干态隐形传态基准函数。

Conclusion: 在非均匀先验分布下，宣告量子优势所需的保真度阈值可能远高于传统均匀分布假设，因此实际实验需根据输入分布特性选择正确的基准。

Abstract: Quantum teleportation should surpass maximum fidelity thresholds possible with local measurements and classical communications. Benchmarks have been established when states are drawn from a uniform distribution of qubits or coherent states of harmonic oscillators. These are not applicable when there is prior knowledge about the input that skews the distribution. We determine the highest mean fidelity achievable for teleportation of states without using entanglement but drawn from a non-uniform prior distribution, specifically a von Mises - Fisher distribution. Using Bayesian methods, we derive benchmark functions for single qubit teleportation that utilize projective measurement on specific axes on the Bloch sphere. We demonstrate that similar results can be achieved with a coherent spin positive operator-valued measure (POVM), based upon heterodyne detection. These methods are then used to derive corresponding benchmarks for teleporting multi-qubit systems that we model as spin coherent states, covering the full range of particle number $N\in [1, \infty)$. The results show that fidelity thresholds to declare quantum advantage can be significantly higher than is typically assumed with uniform distribution.

</details>


### [95] [Singularizing preserving countable additivity quantum channels on quantum measurable cardinals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24923)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究von Neumann代数上由算子群通过Pettis积分平均诱导的一类量子信道的结构与动力学性质，建立了信道保持σ-可加性并展现奇异化性质的充分条件，结合Ulam实值可测基数理论揭示了将正态转化为奇异但严格σ-可加态的新现象，并分析了连续统基数对态纯度的约束，扩展至群上不变测度及其酉表示，证明了Cesàro平均在强算子拓扑下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子信道在von Neumann代数框架下由算子群平均诱导的结构性质，揭示正态与奇异态之间转化的可能性与约束条件。

Method: 利用Pettis积分定义算子群平均诱导的量子信道，借助经典的Yosida-Hewitt分解分析信道结构，结合Ulam实值可测基数理论建立充分条件，并应用连续统基数假设研究态纯度保持的约束。

Result: 1. 建立了信道保持σ-可加性并展现奇异化性质的充分条件；2. 证明了存在量子信道能将正态转化为奇异但严格σ-可加态；3. 揭示了连续统基数对态纯度保持的结构性约束；4. 证明了群上不变测度及其酉表示的Cesàro平均在强算子拓扑下收敛。

Conclusion: 本文建立的框架不仅发现了量子信道中正态到奇异态转化的新现象，还揭示了集合论性质（如Ulam基数、连续统基数）与量子信息结构之间的深刻联系，为理解量子信道中态的奇异行为提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We investigate the structural and dynamical properties of a class of quantum channels on von Neumann algebras induced by averaging over operator groups via the Pettis integral.
  Utilizing the classical Yosida--Hewitt decomposition, we focus on the interplay between the set-theoretic properties of the underlying space and the topological nature of the resulting quantum states.
  We establish a suffucient condition under which a channel preserves $σ$-additivity and exhibits a singularizing property, completely suppressing the normal component of any incoming state.
  In conjunction with the theory of Ulam real-valued measurable cardinals, this framework reveals a novel phenomenon: the existence of quantum channels that transform normal states into singular yet strictly $σ$-additive states.
  Furthermore, we analyze the structural constraints on the preservation of state purity imposed by the cardinality of the continuum, and extend our constructions to invariant measures on groups and their unitary representations, establishing the convergence of their Cesàro averages in the strong operator topology.

</details>


### [96] [Towards a nuclear isomer quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24935)
*Ying-Bo Gao,Fu-Quan Dou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出核同质异能量子电池（NIQBs）设计，利用原子核中的高能、超稳定核能级进行储能，相比传统原子量子电池，储能能量提升10¹-10⁶倍，平均充电功率提升10⁶-10¹¹倍，寿命范围从微秒到10⁵年，且大多数NIQBs可实现完全能量提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子电池研究多聚焦于原子或分子系统，其能级能量低且寿命短，而原子核中高能、超稳定的核能级（如同质异能态）尚未被用于储能，这是一个关键但未被解决的挑战。

Method: 以包含核同质异能态的两能级和三能级原子核作为储能单元，利用X射线自由电子激光（XFEL）与核系统的相互作用驱动充电动力学过程。

Result: 相比原子系统量子电池，NIQBs的储能能量提升10¹-10⁶倍，平均充电功率提升10⁶-10¹¹倍，寿命范围从微秒延伸至10⁵年；绝大多数NIQBs的激发态寿命超过激光-核相互作用时间，自发辐射可忽略，实现完全能量提取。

Conclusion: 提出的NIQBs框架与多种核系统兼容，可根据不同运行条件定制核的选择，为实现高性能、高储能效率的量子电池提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum batteries (QBs) -- quantum devices governed by the principles of quantum mechanics -- hold great promise for next-generation energy storage. However, most existing research efforts focus on atomic or molecular systems featuring low-energy and short-lived energy levels, leaving the exploitation of high-energy, ultra-stable nuclear energy levels for energy storage as a critical unaddressed challenge. Here, we propose an innovative design of referred to as nuclear isomer quantum batteries (NIQBs), whose energy storage unit is typically composed of two-level and three-level nuclei incorporating nuclear isomers. The charging dynamics is driven by the interaction between the nuclear system and x-ray free electron laser (XFEL). Compared to QBs based on atomic systems, NIQBs deliver substantial performance enhancements, with stored energy and average charging power enhanced by factors of $10^{1}$--$10^{6}$ and $10^{6}$--$10^{11}$, respectively, and a markedly extended lifetime range spanning from microseconds to $10^5$ years. Notably, the vast majority of NIQBs enable complete energy extraction as their excited-state lifetimes exceed the laser-nuclear interaction time, rendering spontaneous emission negligible. The proposed NIQBs framework is compatible with diverse nuclear systems, enabling tailored nucleus selection for varied operating conditions. Our results provide a feasible pathway toward realizing high-performance QBs with superior energy-storage efficiency.

</details>


### [97] [Structural constraint on delayed-choice quantum eraser architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24994)
*Chakir Fikri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过引入一个简单的概率约束，证明了在理想化延迟选择量子擦除（DCQE）实验中，四个直观性质——选择统计独立性、无损耗、基于选择的条件确定性路由、以及不同的条件检测分布——无法同时成立。这种不相容性为DCQE方案提供了清晰的分类，并阐明了条件干涉图样如何在不诉诸奇异机制的情况下产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCQE实验常被用来挑战经典因果直觉，但其中的后选择机制和结构特征缺乏统一框架分析。

Method: 引入一个简单的概率约束，分析四个直观性质之间的逻辑关系与兼容性。

Result: 四个直观性质无法同时满足，提供了DCQE方案的透明分类。

Conclusion: DCQE中的条件干涉图样无需借用奇异机制即可得到合理解释。

Abstract: Delayed-choice quantum eraser (DCQE) experiments are often presented as challenging classical causal intuitions by correlating detection events with choices implemented at later times. While it is well understood that post-selection plays a crucial role in producing the observed interference patterns, the structural features underlying such correlations are rarely analyzed within a unified framework. In this work, we introduce a simple probabilistic constraint applicable to idealized DCQE architectures. We show that four intuitive properties -- statistical independence of the choice, absence of losses, deterministic routing conditioned on the choice, and distinct conditional detection distributions -- cannot be simultaneously satisfied. This incompatibility provides a transparent classification of DCQE schemes and clarifies how conditional interference patterns arise without invoking exotic mechanisms.

</details>


### [98] [Nonreciprocal Landau-Zener Tunneling in the Presence of Heat-Bath-Induced Colored Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25008)
*Yi Cao,Jie Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了热浴诱导的有色噪声对非互易Landau-Zener隧穿的影响，发现噪声引发能级随机涨落，足够大的非互易参数可导致多个异常点（exceptional points）的出现，且有色噪声在非绝热扫描下增强隧穿概率，在绝热极限下破坏隧穿互易性，大非互易参数时绝热隧穿概率由异常点主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究热浴诱导的有色噪声对非互易Landau-Zener隧穿过程的影响机制，以及噪声与非互易性之间的耦合效应。

Method: 数值计算了瞬时能级结构，分析了噪声引起的随机涨落和异常点涌现；解析上利用Weber函数推导了无噪声情况的精确隧穿概率表达式，采用Wiener-Hermite展开方法得到了白噪声极限下的近似解。

Result: 有色噪声在非绝热扫描下增强隧穿概率；在绝热极限下破坏隧穿互易性；大非互易参数时绝热隧穿概率对噪声不敏感，由异常点决定；绘制了不同噪声振幅和相关时间下的隧穿概率相图。

Conclusion: 噪声与非互易性共同调控Landau-Zener隧穿行为，异常点成为大非互易参数下决定绝热隧穿概率的关键因素。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the effects of heat-bath-induced colored noise on nonreciprocal Landau-Zener tunneling. We perform numerical calculations of the instantaneous energy levels and find that noise induces stochastic fluctuations in these levels. Beyond such fluctuations, sufficiently large nonreciprocity parameters enable noise to further trigger the emergence of multiple exceptional points. The corresponding tunneling probability is significantly modified by colored noise. On the one hand, colored noise generally enhances the tunneling probability under nonadiabatic sweeping. On the other hand, it can break the reciprocity of Landau-Zener tunneling in the adiabatic limit. In particular, for large nonreciprocity parameters, the adiabatic tunneling probability is insensitive to noise and determined by exceptional points. We plot the phase diagram of tunneling probability as a function of the sweep rate and nonreciprocity parameter, for different amplitudes and correlation times of colored noise. Analytically, we derive the exact expression for the tunneling probability in the absence of noise and an approximate solution in the white-noise limit, employing Weber functions and the Wiener-Hermite expansion method. Some implications of our theory are discussed.

</details>


### [99] [QML-PipeGuard: Drift-Aware Behavioral Fingerprinting for Quantum Machine Learning Pipeline Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25066)
*Esra Yeniaras*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QML-PipeGuard，一个基于合约的量子机器学习管道完整性验证框架，可同时检测硬件自然漂移和恶意通道替换攻击，并在IBM量子处理器上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习正进入受监管行业，面临两个实际威胁：硬件校准间的通道级噪声漂移，以及攻击者利用执行环境控制权替换量子通道为行为相似但数学不同的通道。现有QML验证工作（脉冲级噪声、输入漂移、鲁棒性、设备身份）均未覆盖这两类问题。

Method: 提出QML-PipeGuard框架：通过断层扫描结构化的测量族获取可观测期望值向量作为行为指纹，运行两种模式——漂移感知监控（吸收良性校准变化）和对抗检测（检测通道替换违反信息完备可观测量合约）。贡献包括：管道组合处理编码器-ansatz-测量通道、QML特定威胁模型（单比特Pauli族的紧框架界C=√3）、有限样本复杂度界、以及分离对抗与自然漂移的容差分解。

Result: 在IBM Heron r2处理器（ibm_fez）上对两比特QSVM管道进行端到端验证，并在噪声匹配模拟器上验证样本复杂度。测量预算约1.4e4次射击（单批次作业），恶意通道被大安全裕度检测到同时规避弱合约，典型硬件漂移在容差范围内。

Conclusion: QML-PipeGuard为QML管道运行时完整性提供了统一的数学框架，有效区分自然漂移与恶意攻击，满足实际部署的测量预算约束。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is moving from research prototypes to deployed cloud services. As QML enters regulated industries, the integrity of the quantum stage becomes a practical concern on two fronts: noisy hardware drifts at the channel level between recalibrations, and an adversary with control over the execution environment can substitute the declared quantum channel with a behaviorally similar but mathematically distinct one. Neither concern is covered by existing QML verification work on pulse-level noise, input drift, input-perturbation robustness, or device identity. We introduce QML-PipeGuard, a contract-based framework addressing both concerns under a single mathematical machinery. It characterizes a QML pipeline at runtime by its behavioral fingerprint, the vector of observable expectation values under a tomographically structured measurement family, and operates in two modes: drift-aware monitoring that absorbs benign calibration changes within a calibrated tolerance, and adversarial detection that catches channel substitution as a violation of an informationally complete observable contract. The framework contributes a pipeline-composition treatment of the encoder-ansatz-measurement channel with a QML-specific threat model (tight frame-bound C=sqrt(3) for the single-qubit Pauli family), a finite-shot sample-complexity bound, and a tolerance decomposition separating adversarial and natural-drift contributions. We validate the framework end-to-end on a two-qubit QSVM pipeline on the IBM Heron r2 processor (ibm_fez), with a sample-complexity validation on a noise-matched simulator. The prescribed measurement budget (about 1.4e4 shots) fits in a single batched job, the sneaky channel is detected with a wide safety margin while evading the weak contract, and the typical hardware drift sits within tolerance.

</details>


### [100] [A framework for the benchmarking of transport-induced excitations in shuttling-based ion-trap quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25118)
*Rodrigo Munoz,Phil Nuschke,Teresa Meiners,Brigitte Kaune,Christian Ospelkaus*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种理论数值框架，将离子传输协议分解为基本操作并分别表征其加热性能，通过代数表达式组合得到全局结果。应用于8量子比特处理器设计，展示了如何将运动操作代价作为成本函数整合到编译器中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离子阱量子处理器中，离子在不同区域间的传输（shuttling）会激发离子运动态，导致加热和退相干。当前方法需要模拟每条完整离子轨迹的整个传输协议，计算成本高。因此需要一种更高效的方法来分析和预测传输对离子运动态的影响。

Method: 将传输协议分解为基本操作（primitive operations），分别表征每个基本操作的加热性能，然后通过代数表达式组合得到全局传输协议的加热结果。避免了全轨迹模拟的需求。以8量子比特全连通处理器设计（基于线性传输和swap操作）为例进行了演示。

Result: 成功将传输协议的加热效应分解为基本操作的代数组合，无需对每条完整离子轨迹进行全模拟。在8量子比特处理器设计中验证了方法的可行性，并展示了如何将运动操作的代价作为成本函数集成到编译器层面。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了评估传输协议对离子运动态影响的计算复杂度，使得在编译器层面优化离子传输路径以最小化加热效应成为可能，对大规模离子阱量子处理器的设计和运行具有重要意义。

Abstract: We develop a theoretical and numerical framework to analyze the effect of transport on the motional states of ions in a trapped-ion quantum processor. We decompose the shuttling protocol into primitive operations and characterize these in terms of their heating performance. Instead of having to simulate the whole transport protocol for each complete ion trajectory, the method allows us to determine the heating properties of each primitive operation separately and obtain the global result through an algebraic expression. We demonstrate our method by applying it to an 8-qubit quantum processor design based on linear transport and swap operations for all-to-all connectivity. We show how to incorporate the price of motional operations at the level of the compiler as a cost function.

</details>


### [101] [Transition from Collective to Local Radial Motional Modes in a Tapered Paul Trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25125)
*Manika Bhardwaj,Moritz Göb,Bogomila S. Nikolova,Bernd Bauerhenne,Peter A. Ivanov,Kilian Singer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了锥形保罗阱中径向集体振荡模式向个体振荡的转变，并在过渡区域进行了详细的实验与理论比较，为研究离子阱中的热传输和缺陷形成提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解耦合失谐振子中从集体振荡到个体振荡的转变机制，特别是在锥形保罗阱中的径向模式转换，以服务于量子信息处理和热传输、缺陷形成等物理现象的研究。

Method: 采用锥形保罗阱实验平台，通过改变轴向约束强度，系统研究了径向本征模式从集体行为到个体行为的转变过程，并将实验结果与理论预测进行详细对比。

Result: 实验成功观测了从集体径向模式（强轴向约束）到个体径向振荡（弱轴向约束）的转变，在过渡区域详细表征了本征模行为，实验结果与理论预测吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了锥形保罗阱中径向振荡模式的转变特性，为后续探索离子阱中修正热传输现象和缺陷形成提供了实验基础和理论支持。

Abstract: With coupled detuned oscillators, either individual or collective oscillations are observable. The latter is used in quantum information processing in linear Paul traps. Here, we study the transition from collective radial modes at stronger axial confinements into individual radial oscillations at low axial confinements in a tapered Paul trap. The eigenmodes are experimentally studied in detail in the transition regime and compared with theoretical predictions. The features studied will enable investigation of modified heat transport phenomena and defect formation in trapped ions.

</details>


### [102] [Dispersive readout of cavity-coupled solid-state sensor with near-unity readout fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25152)
*Hanfeng Wang,Shuang Wu,Matthew E. Trusheim,Avetik Harutyunyan,Dirk R. Englund*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于氮空位(NV)中心与微波腔色散耦合的量子读取技术，克服了传统连续波腔量子电动力学(cQED)方法中的自旋饱和与功率展宽限制，实现了接近自旋投影噪声极限的飞特斯拉级磁灵敏度，并展示了随自旋数N呈1/N改善的灵敏度标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于NV色心集合的连续波cQED读出方法受自旋饱和与功率展宽限制，远未达到自旋投影噪声极限，亟需一种新的读取方案来突破这些基本限制，实现更高灵敏度的固态量子传感。

Method: 本文引入了一种色散cQED读出技术，建立了描述色散相互作用的全面理论框架，并分析了强耦合NV-腔系统的时间域动力学特性。

Result: 利用商用金刚石NV色心集合，实现了接近单位逆读取保真度和飞特斯拉级灵敏度；色散读出灵敏度随自旋数N呈1/N标度改善，为逼近标准量子极限提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 色散cQED读出技术有效克服了自旋饱和与功率展宽限制，使NV集合固态传感器接近自旋投影噪声极限，为高精度量子计量学提供了重要的技术突破。

Abstract: Solid-state quantum sensors based on ensembles of nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond have emerged as powerful platforms for high-precision metrology. Coupling the NV ensemble to a microwave cavity mode in a cavity quantum electrodynamics (cQED) configuration enables spin readout that surpasses the limitations of conventional optical detection, achieving sub-picotesla magnetic sensitivities. However, existing continuous-wave cQED approaches remain far from the intrinsic spin-projection-noise limit due to spin saturation and power broadening. Here, we introduce a dispersive cQED readout technique to overcome these fundamental limitations in NV ensemble sensing. We develop a comprehensive theoretical framework describing the dispersive interaction and analyze the time-domain dynamics of a strongly-coupled NV-cavity system. Our results indicate near-unity inverse readout fidelity and femtotesla-level sensitivity using a commercially available diamond NV ensemble. Importantly, the dispersive readout exhibits a distinct sensitivity scaling that improves as 1/N with increasing number of spins N, providing a practical pathway toward approaching the standard quantum limit for solid-state spin-ensemble sensors.

</details>


### [103] [Causal Order Cannot Be An Observable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25302)
*Declan Maguire,Fabio Costa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将可观测量定义为基于判别任务的完全操作性定义，证明了因果顺序不能被视为一种可观测量，因为因果类虽然满足尖锐性（条件1），但违反了条件2和条件3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子因果结构能否被类比为可观测量进行测量，即检验因果顺序是否可以被视为一种可观测的物理量。

Method: 构建基于判别任务的可观测量操作性定义，提出三个条件（尖锐性、成对可区分蕴含联合可区分、类与实体可区分蕴含类整体与实体可区分），并将此定义应用于量子过程，检验具有严格因果顺序的因果类是否满足这些条件。

Result: 因果类满足条件1（尖锐性），但存在违反条件2和条件3的情况，表明因果顺序不能被视为可观测量。文献中声称相反结论的工作预设了等价于条件2和条件3的特殊条件。

Conclusion: 因果顺序不可被解释为一种可观测量，其与可观测量之间的类比存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Recent developments in the formalisation of quantum causal structures have made it possible to test and compare hypotheses about causal structure empirically, rather than being a-priori assumptions. Such differences in causal structure may be leveraged to distinguish between the processes they belong to, akin to distinguishing between quantum states known to belong to different eigenspaces of an observable. So how far this analogy can be pushed? Can causal order be interpreted as a kind of observable? Can it be measured?
  To this end we construct a completely operational definition of observables in terms of `discrimination tasks'. This begins with some base set of classes (analogous to a quantum observable's eigenspaces) before we impose three conditions on how these classes relate under discrimination tasks. These conditions recover the properties of a standard observable, letting us apply this definition to non-state entities such as quantum processes, which naturally encode causal structure.
  These conditions can be described in plain language as (1) `members of any class are perfectly distinguishable from members of any other' (i.e. our classes are `sharp'), (2) `entities perfectly distinguishable pairwise are all perfectly distinguishable by a joint intervention', and (3) `if all members of a class are perfectly distinguishable from an entity, then the whole class is perfectly distinguishable from it jointly'.
  By analysing classes of quantum processes having strict causal orders, we find causal classes which are sharp but violate condition 2, and classes which violate condition 3. This implies that causal order cannot be an observable.
  We note cases in the literature which claim to prove the opposite, and discuss them. We find their analyses presume special circumstances equivalent to conditions (2) and (3), and so do not contradict our own findings.

</details>


### [104] [Boson Sampling as a Probe of Chaotic and Integrable Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25398)
*Yuancheng Zhan,Khen Cohen,Norman T. W. Koo,Kian Hwee Lim,Hui Zhang,Lingxiao Wan,Sanghoon Chae,Ai Qun Liu,Victor M Bastidas,Yaron Oz,Leong-Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将Fock态玻色采样作为量子混沌探针，并在可编程硅基量子光子芯片上实验实现了这一框架，首次在集成光子平台上演示了基于玻色采样的量子混沌探测，实验结果与理论预测高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成光子学在量子信息领域优势显著，但量子混沌在集成光子平台上的探测仍然几乎未被探索，因为可编程光子动力学与已有的混沌诊断方法之间缺乏明确的联系。

Method: 利用多光子干涉对单粒子幺正动力学随机矩阵性质的敏感性，将Fock态玻色采样确立为量子混沌的实用探针；设计并制备了可编程硅基量子光子芯片，实验实现了三种互补探针：Porter-Thomas统计距离、Shannon熵和与Out-of-Time-Ordered-Correlator等价的观测量。

Result: 实验结果表明，三种探针均与理论预测高度一致，并能清晰区分混沌动力学与可积动力学。

Conclusion: 该工作为在可编程光子平台上研究复杂量子动力学提供了一条可扩展的路径，同时利用多光子干涉和复杂输出统计发挥了玻色采样的内在优势。

Abstract: Quantum technologies have become a powerful paradigm for quantum information and simulation, while quantum chaos plays a key role in understanding complex quantum dynamics. Integrated photonics offers unique advantages for quantum applications, including high-speed operation, scalability, and programmable unitary transformations. However, probing quantum chaos on integrated photonic platforms remains largely unexplored because a clear connection between programmable photonic dynamics and established chaos diagnostics is still lacking. In this work, we establish Fock-state boson sampling as a practical probe of quantum chaos by exploiting the sensitivity of multiphoton interference to the random-matrix properties of underlying single-particle unitary dynamics. More importantly, we design and fabricate a programmable silicon quantum photonic chip to experimentally implement this framework, achieving the first integrated-photonic demonstration of quantum-chaos probes based on boson sampling. Experimental results show that the three complementary probes proposed in this work, namely the distance to Porter-Thomas statistics, Shannon entropy, and Out-of-Time-Ordered-Correlator-equivalent observables, exhibit close agreement with theoretical predictions and consistently distinguish chaotic and integrable dynamics. Our work provides a scalable route for investigating complex quantum dynamics on programmable photonic platforms while leveraging the intrinsic advantages of boson sampling through multiphoton interference and complex output statistics.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Logical Circuits: Hardware-Aware Analysis of Expressibility and Trainability in Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25552)
*Muhammad Kashif,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了硬件感知的量子电路编译（transpilation）对变分量子算法中参数化量子电路的可表达性和可训练性的影响，发现编译会显著改变电路特性，且逻辑层面的分析无法可靠预测硬件层面的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常在逻辑电路层面评估变分量子算法的可表达性和可训练性，隐含假设设计的参数化量子电路在硬件执行时保持不变。然而实际执行中，硬件感知的编译过程会通过量子比特映射、路由和基分解修改电路结构，可能改变电路行为，因此需要分析这种影响。

Method: 对比逻辑电路与编译后电路，跨越多类拟设族、不同量子比特数和电路深度，使用保真度KL散度衡量可表达性，梯度方差量化可训练性，进行系统化的硬件感知分析。

Result: 编译相当于隐式架构扰动，产生显著的拟设依赖效应：可表达性偏差最高超过125%，可训练性变化最高达25%。结构化拟设通常更鲁棒，高度纠缠的架构对编译诱导的变换更敏感。编译会改变通常假设的可表达性-可训练性权衡关系。

Conclusion: 逻辑层面的分析无法可靠预测硬件层面的行为，强调了硬件感知评估对于准确表征变分量子算法的重要性。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) rely on parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs), whose performance is governed by expressibility and trainability. Existing studies typically evaluate these properties at the logical circuit level, implicitly assuming that designed PQCs remain unchanged during hardware execution. In practice, however, hardware-aware transpilation modifies circuit structure through qubit mapping, routing, and basis decomposition, potentially altering PQC behavior. In this paper, we perform a systematic hardware-aware analysis of expressibility and trainability by comparing logical and transpiled PQCs across multiple ansatz families, qubit counts, and circuit depths. Expressibility is measured using fidelity-based KL divergence, while trainability is quantified through gradient variance. Our results show that transpilation acts as an implicit architectural perturbation, producing strongly ansatz-dependent effects. Expressibility deviations exceed upto 125% in some cases, while trainability variations reach up to 25%. Structured ansatzes are generally more robust, whereas highly entangled architectures are more sensitive to transpilation-induced transformations. We further show that transpilation can alter the commonly assumed expressibility-trainability trade-off, demonstrating that logical-level analyses may not reliably predict hardware-level behavior. These findings highlight the importance of hardware-aware evaluation for accurate characterization of VQAs.

</details>


### [106] [Dissipative Time Quasicrystals from Multilevel Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25667)
*Kang Shen,Xiangming Hu,Fei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了多能级干涉可以自发产生耗散时间准晶，无需外部准周期驱动，实现了连续时间平移对称性的自发破缺到准周期振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越时间晶体中周期性有序的新机制，寻找在耗散多体系统中自发产生时间准周期有序的途径。

Method: 构建了一个集体驱动-耗散的四能级系统（两个简并激发态和两个简并基态），在热力学极限下分析其平均场动力学，通过不可比频率的双环面流实现准周期序参量。

Result: 系统动力学退化为二维环面上的非有理流，产生具有两个不可比基频的离散谱准周期序参量；最大李雅普诺夫指数为零，表明非线性自洽动力学是非混沌的。

Conclusion: 建立了一种最小化的干涉诱导时间准周期有序机制，为多能级系统中更高维准周期动力学开辟了道路。

Abstract: Boundary time crystals exhibit spontaneous breaking of continuous time-translation symmetry through persistent periodic oscillations in driven-dissipative many-body systems. Here, we show that multilevel interference provides a natural route beyond periodic order, enabling dissipative time quasicrystals without externally imposed quasiperiodic driving. We consider a collectively driven-dissipative four-level ensemble with two degenerate excited states and two degenerate ground states. In the thermodynamic limit, the exact mean-field dynamics reduces to an irrational flow on a two-dimensional torus, yielding quasiperiodic order parameters with discrete spectra generated by two incommensurate fundamental frequencies. Vanishing maximal Lyapunov exponents demonstrate that the nonlinear self-consistent dynamics remains nonchaotic. Our results establish a minimal interference-induced mechanism for time-quasiperiodic order and open a route toward higher-dimensional quasiperiodic dynamics in multilevel systems.

</details>


### [107] [Homomorphic Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25692)
*Kornikar Sen,Miguel A. Martin-Delgado*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子同态加密与量子纠错码之间的代数兼容性，给出了稳定子码与受限横向块-Pauli掩码兼容的充要条件，并探讨了非Clifford门实现下的两种解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在云端量子计算中，需要同时保护量子数据免受未授权访问（加密）和环境噪声（纠错）的影响，因此需要研究量子同态加密与量子纠错在代数结构上的兼容性。

Method: 建立了[[n,1,d]]稳定子码与受限横向块-Pauli掩码 U_enc(a,b)=(X^aZ^b)^⊗n 兼容的充要条件，该条件可直接推广到[[n,k,d]]码。将此条件应用于比特翻转码、Shor码、CSS码以及三维颜色码。针对非Clifford门（T门）的挑战，提出了两条路径：利用三正交码实现横向T型逻辑门（辅以Clifford纠正），以及逻辑门掩码方法。

Result: 给出了稳定子码与同态加密兼容的充要判据；验证了比特翻转码和Shor码满足条件；发现了Shor码缺乏朴素横向T门的问题；提出了两种绕过该障碍的方案。

Conclusion: 本文的工作将码空间兼容性与完整的密码学安全证明分离开来，为云端量子计算中组合纠错与同态处理提供了明确的判据。

Abstract: Homomorphic quantum error correction aims to protect quantum data against both unauthorized access and environmental noise during server-based processing. We investigate the algebraic compatibility between quantum homomorphic encryption and quantum error correction, determining precise conditions under which encrypted encoded states remain inside the relevant code space during storage and computation. Our work establishes a necessary and sufficient criterion for an $[[n,1,d]]$ stabilizer code to remain compatible with the restricted transversal block-Pauli masking $U_{\rm enc}(a,b)=(X^aZ^b)^{\otimes n}$, stated explicitly for $[[n,1,d]]$ codes and extending directly to code-space preservation for $[[n,k,d]]$ codes. We verify this condition for standard examples (bit-flip and Shor codes, with the phase-flip repetition code following analogously), derive a practical criterion for Calderbank-Shor-Steane codes, and extend the analysis to three-dimensional color codes. A critical challenge emerges for non-Clifford gate implementation: the Shor code lacks a naive transversal $T$-gate implementation of the desired logical operation on encrypted encoded data. We present two routes around this obstruction. First, suitable triorthogonal codes admit transversal $T$-type logical implementations, up to Clifford corrections. Second, logical-gate masking gives code-space compatibility for arbitrary stabilizer codes, provided that suitable unitary representatives of the required logical gates are available. These results separate code-space compatibility from a full cryptographic security proof and provide explicit criteria for combining error correction with homomorphic processing in cloud quantum computing.

</details>


### [108] [Q-LEAK: Quantum-Based LEAKage Verification for Side-Channel Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25728)
*Walid El Maouaki,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-LEAK，一种基于Grover算法的量子辅助验证方法，用于解决密码算法中功耗侧信道泄漏的SAT验证可扩展性问题。该方法将单比特双迹泄漏模型编码为CNF公式，利用量子幅度放大在O(√N)次预言调用中搜索满足赋值。在5-7变量基准测试中，Q-LEAK在1-4次尝试内恢复满足赋值，标记比特串概率超过20%，并在真实量子硬件上成功找到经典验证的SAT赋值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于SAT的经典方法在XOR密集型、时间展开的密码电路上难以扩展，无法有效验证功耗侧信道泄漏及其防护措施。

Method: Q-LEAK方法：1) 构建紧凑CNF公式，建模两迹条件下单比特泄漏，关联密钥依赖与状态演化；2) 将每个CNF编译为量子预言；3) 应用Grover算法的幅度放大，以O(√N)次预言调用搜索满足赋值。

Result: 在5-7变量无噪声基准测试中，Q-LEAK在1-4次尝试内恢复满足赋值，标记比特串概率超过20%。在真实量子硬件上，尽管存在噪声，仍能找到至少一个经典验证的SAT赋值。

Conclusion: Q-LEAK展示了量子辅助验证侧信道防护的潜在路径，在解决经典SAT方法可扩展性瓶颈方面具有前景，但实际资源限制仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Formal verification of power side-channel leakage and its countermeasures in cryptographic algorithms is challenging, as SAT-based methods fail to scale on XOR-heavy, time-unrolled cryptographic circuits with realistic leakage models. We construct compact Conjunctive Normal Form (CNF) cases modeling one-bit leakage under two-trace conditions, linking key dependence and state evolution. Classical solvers quickly reach complexity limits, so we propose Q-LEAK, a quantum-based verification approach using Grover's algorithm, compiling each CNF into an oracle and applying amplitude amplification to search in O(sqrt(N)) oracle calls, with oracles that encode the two-trace leakage predicate and the CNF constraints. Benchmarking against classical SAT shows both potential gains and practical resource limits. In noiseless tests on 5-7 variable benchmarks, Q-LEAK consistently recovered a satisfying assignment within 1-4 tries, with marked bitstrings amplified clearly above the background distribution, exceeding 20 percent probability. The evaluation of Q-LEAK on real quantum hardware revealed at least one classically verified SAT assignment, despite the presence of noise. These results point to a potential path toward quantum-assisted verification of side-channel protections.

</details>


### [109] [Wave-particle duality of unpolarized photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25738)
*Naofumi Abe,Keiichi Edamatsu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对波粒二象性关系$V^2+D^2 \leq 1$在混合态which-way标记下无法饱和的问题，提出了一个基于纯化框架的广义可分辨度$D$，使其能够正确量化路径信息，并在实验中利用完全混合态（非偏振）单光子验证了饱和的波粒二象性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的波粒二象性关系$V^2+D^2 \leq 1$仅在纯态which-way标记下饱和，当标记处于混合态时，常规定义的可分辨度低估了路径信息量，导致不等式无法达到饱和。

Method: 将混合态WWM视为WWM与外部自由度（如环境）纠缠形成的结果，基于纯化框架重新定义可分辨度$D$，使其包含WWM与环境共享的全部路径信息；并证明该$D$可在无需访问环境的情况下实验量化。

Result: 利用完全混合态（非偏振态）的真实单光子，实验测量了干涉条纹可见度$V$和广义可分辨度$D$，验证了饱和的波粒二象性关系。

Conclusion: 提出的广义可分辨度$D$能够正确量化混合态WWM中的路径信息，使波粒二象性关系对所有纯态和混合态WWM均能饱和，拓展了波粒二象性的适用范围。

Abstract: Photons in a two-path interferometer best embody wave-particle duality (WPD), which is a core concept of quantum theory. So far, the WPD relation is commonly written as $V^2+D^2 \leq 1$, where $V$ is the interference fringe visibility and $D$ is path distinguishability, i.e., the distinguishability of which path a photon passed. This inequality is saturated only when the which-way marker (WWM), which embodies which-path information (WPI) via an internal degree of freedom of photons, such as polarization, is in a pure state. For mixed-state WWM, conventionally defined distinguishability underestimates the amount of WPI and thus does not saturate the WPD relation. Here, we introduce a generalized measure of distinguishability $D$ that properly quantifies the WPI and saturates the WPD relation for all pure- and mixed-state WWM within a purification-based framework. To this end, mixed-state WWM is treated as a result of entanglement formation between the WWM and an external degree of freedom, e.g., environment, and $D$ is defined so that it incorporates the total WPI shared between the WWM and the environment. We show that $D$ thus defined is experimentally quantifiable, independently of $V$, without access to the environment. We experimentally evaluate $V$ and $D$ using true single photons generated in the completely mixed (unpolarized) state, and thus verify the saturated WPD relation.

</details>


### [110] [QGCL: Quantum-Guided Clause Learning for Cryptanalytic SAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25756)
*Walid El Maouaki,Alberto Marchisio,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QGCL框架，将Grover搜索与CDCL求解器结合，用于AES功耗旁路攻击的SAT求解，在较大规模实例上冲突数量减少高达86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功耗旁路攻击中，将含噪泄漏观测转化为经过验证的AES-128密钥是一个困难的组合搜索问题；现有SAT辅助的旁路密码分析虽能编码AES语义和泄漏谓词为CNF，但所生成的CNF公式规模巨大，完整Grover搜索远超当前量子硬件能力。

Method: 提出QGCL框架：在CDCL冲突核心周围动态提取小子公式，仅对这些小子公式调用Grover搜索；量子子求解器返回候选赋值和违反分数以偏置分支启发式，而SAT/UNSAT最终判定和密钥验证保持经典计算。

Result: 在AES功耗旁路SAT实例上评估，较经典冲突学习基线，较难实例上冲突减少高达86%；对Grover oracle调用次数和子问题大小的参数扫描确定了适度量子资源即可捕获大部分改进的区间。

Conclusion: QGCL框架通过混合量子-经典方式，在有限量子资源下有效加速AES旁路攻击中的SAT求解，为近期的量子辅助密码分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Power side-channel attacks on AES exploit data-dependent physical leakage to recover secret keys, but turning noisy leakage observations into a verified AES-128 key remains a hard combinational search problem. SAT-assisted power side-channel cryptanalysis addresses this challenge by encoding AES semantics, key constraints, plaintext/ciphertext consistency, and leakage predicates as CNF, so that candidate keys must satisfy the exact cryptographic specification. These cryptanalytic SAT formulas are large and highly structured; our largest controlled AES-oriented power-SCA instances contain up to 39,389 variables and 137,712 clauses, making a full-formula Grover search well beyond the scale studied here and beyond currently practical near-term implementations. We propose QGCL, a Quantum-Guided Conflict-Driven Clause Learning (CDCL) framework in which Grover search is invoked only on small subformulas extracted dynamically around CDCL conflict cores. The quantum subsolver returns candidate assignments and violation scores that bias branching heuristics, while final SAT/UNSAT decisions and key verification remain classical. We evaluate QGCL on AES-oriented cryptanalytic SAT instances derived from power side-channel CNFs with leakage-derived hint configurations, measuring conflicts, restarts, decisions, and propagations. The experiments show consistent reductions in these solver-internal statistics on harder instances, with up to an 86% reduction in conflicts compared with the classical conflict-learning baseline. Parameter sweeps over the number of Grover oracle calls and the subproblem size identify a regime in which a modest quantum resource allocation captures most of the observed improvement.

</details>


### [111] [Thermalization in Spatially Extended Open Quantum Systems: Local versus Global Markovian Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25760)
*Jorge Tabanera-Bravo,Massimiliano Esposito,Felipe Barra,Juan M. R. Parrondo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了与热库局部耦合的量子比特链动力学，通过重复碰撞模型推导出热力学一致的局域Lindblad方程，并揭示了全局Lindblad方程（基于弱耦合理论中的世俗近似）与文献中常用但缺乏热力学一致性的局域耗散模型之间的交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中常用的局域耗散模型通常无法保证热力学一致性，而全局Lindblad方程虽热力学一致但适用范围有限。本文旨在构建一种在短时保持局域性同时满足热力学一致性的动力学框架，弥合这两类模型之间的鸿沟。

Method: 通过重复碰撞模型（repeated collisions）将量子比特链局部耦合到热库，推导出相应Lindblad方程，分析其热力学一致性条件，并与世俗近似下的全局Lindblad方程及传统局域耗散模型进行比较。

Result: 在适当条件下，所推导的Lindblad方程在短时保持局域性，同时能够驱动系统趋向热平衡（热力学一致）。该框架揭示了全局Lindblad方程与局域耗散模型之间存在一个交叉区域。

Conclusion: 通过重复碰撞模型可以在局域性和热力学一致性之间取得平衡，为理解开放量子系统中局域与全局耗散动力学的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of a qubit chain locally coupled to a thermal reservoir, modeled through repeated collisions with particles drawn from a heat bath. Under suitable conditions, the resulting Lindblad equation is thermodynamically consistent -- it drives the system toward thermal equilibrium -- while remaining local at short times. This framework reveals a crossover between the global Lindblad equation derived from the secular approximation in weak-coupling theory and the local dissipative models often employed in the literature, which generally fail to ensure thermodynamic consistency.

</details>


### [112] [Q-RAIL: A Reliability-Aware Framework for Quantum Federated Learning on Heterogeneous Noisy Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25783)
*Walid El Maouaki,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Q-RAIL是一种面向异构量子后端的电路感知与校准驱动聚合方法，通过计算客户端有效噪声预算并转化为稳定聚合权重，在NISQ硬件上实现鲁棒的量子联邦学习，在MNIST等基准上较FedAvg显著提升准确率（0.777→0.877）并降低测试损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子联邦学习在NISQ硬件上对后端异构性高度敏感：部分客户端提供信息丰富的更新，而另一些客户端贡献噪声主导的漂移，传统均匀聚合方法无法区分二者，亟需一种能感知硬件差异的聚合机制。

Method: 提出Q-RAIL方法，首先从后端校准元数据和已编译电路统计信息中计算客户端特定的有效噪声预算；然后通过温度缩放、均匀混合和最小权重下限将该预算转化为稳定的聚合权重，实现电路感知的加权聚合。

Result: 在强硬件偏移的MNIST基准上，Q-RAIL将测试准确率从FedAvg的0.777提升至0.877（+10.0点，相对误差减少约44.8%），超越最强wpQFL基线（0.833）；测试损失从0.722降至0.585；测试AUC从0.920升至0.973。在非IID MNIST上达0.813 vs 0.722；在12/12种ansatz/CX-fold应力配置中均优于FedAvg，并在4、10、15量子位设置下表现更优。

Conclusion: 校准驱动的电路感知聚合是实现在异构量子硬件上进行鲁棒量子联邦学习的实用路径。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning (QFL) on NISQ hardware is highly sensitive to backend heterogeneity: some clients contribute informative updates, while others contribute noise-dominated drift that uniform averaging cannot distinguish. We propose Q-RAIL (Quantum Reliability-Aware Federated Inference and Learning), a circuit- and calibration-aware aggregation method for hardware-heterogeneous QFL. Q-RAIL computes a client-specific effective noise budget from backend calibration metadata together with transpiled circuit statistics. This budget is converted into stabilized aggregation weights using temperature scaling, uniform mixing, and a minimum-weight floor. Q-RAIL was evaluated across multiple experimental settings, including an ablation study, and benchmarked against state-of-the-art methods on three datasets: MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and OrganAMNIST. On the primary MNIST benchmark under strong hardware skew, Q-RAIL improves final test accuracy from FedAvg's 0.777 to 0.877, a +10.0-point gain corresponding to about 44.8% relative error reduction, while also exceeding the strongest wpQFL baseline (0.833). At the same time, test loss drops from 0.722 to 0.585, and test AUC rises from 0.920 to 0.973. Under non-IID MNIST, Q-RAIL reaches 0.813 vs 0.722 for FedAvg. It also outperforms FedAvg in 12/12 ansatz/CX-fold stress configurations and remains stronger at 4, 10, and 15 qubit setups. Overall, the results support calibration-driven, circuit-aware aggregation as a practical path toward robust QFL on heterogeneous quantum hardware.

</details>


### [113] [Boundary $0/π$ logical subspace and bulk dynamical probes in flux-controlled anomalous Floquet quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25792)
*WeiCheng Ning,YanSheng Liu,XiaoXue Zhang,XiZheng Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种一维通量控制的反常Floquet量子游走，揭示了其在手征性、拓扑能隙、边界逻辑子空间以及体态动力学探测方面的完整结构，为驱动微结构晶格中的量子游走信息基元提供了对称性保护路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一种通量控制的反常Floquet量子游走，使其同时具备准能量能带、边界模式和实空间体态动力学的统一演化描述，并探索其在拓扑边界逻辑子空间和动力学探测中的应用。

Method: 通过驱动双粒子晶格实现一维通量控制的反常Floquet量子游走，包含依赖于硬币的漂移步和依赖于动量的硬币混合步；利用手征对称性组织拓扑相图，并通过对称时间框架下的平均手征位移和局部频闪响应进行体态动力学探测。

Result: 在手征对称性下，0和π/T能隙携带独立拓扑信息，将(M,φ)平面划分为平凡、仅0、仅π和共存四个拓扑扇区；共存扇区中同一边缘同时存在0模和π模，构成天然边界逻辑子空间，一个Floquet周期实现相对相位操作并产生2T周期响应；体态动力学中，时间框架平均手征位移收敛于两个绕数，π能隙闭合处的奇偶交替效应远强于0能隙闭合处。

Conclusion: 通量控制的反常Floquet量子游走统一了边界逻辑子空间和体态动力学探测，为驱动微结构晶格中基于对称性保护的量子游走信息基元提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We formulate a one-dimensional flux-controlled anomalous Floquet quantum walk and show that it admits a direct microscopic realization in a driven bipartite lattice. The walk consists of a coin-dependent drift step and a momentum-dependent coin mixing step, so the same evolution operator governs quasienergy bands, boundary modes, and bulk dynamics in real space. Because the walk is chiral, the quasienergy gaps at $0$ and $π/T$ carry independent topological information, which organizes trivial, $0$-only, $π$-only, and coexistence sectors in the $(M,φ)$ plane. In the coexistence sector, a $0$ mode and a $π$ mode reside on the same edge and span a natural boundary logical subspace. One Floquet period acts there as a relative phase operation and produces a clear $2T$ response in local boundary observables. In the bulk, the same anomalous Floquet structure is probed dynamically in two complementary ways. Frame-resolved mean chiral displacements approach the two winding numbers in the clean pre-reflection window of the symmetric time frames, while selected benchmark cuts at a representative $0$ gap closing and a representative $π$ gap closing exhibit distinct local stroboscopic responses, with the $π$ gap benchmark showing a much stronger odd-even alternation. The boundary logical subspace and the bulk dynamical probes are therefore organized within one flux-controlled anomalous Floquet quantum walk, suggesting a symmetry-protected route to quantum-walk information primitives in driven microstructured lattices.

</details>


### [114] [Sideband fingerprints of antibunched light in cascaded quantum wave mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25828)
*R. D. Ivanovskikh,W. V. Pogosov,A. A. Elistratov,A. Yu. Dmitriev,T. R. Sabirov,A. V. Vasenin,S. A. Gunin,O. V. Astafiev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了级联源-探针结构中单超导量子比特上的量子波混合现象，推导了弱驱动级联主方程的解析理论，发现在不同滤波极限下，与反聚束源场的多光子吸收相关的边峰会被参数化抑制，数值解验证了解析标度律，提供了反聚束行波微波光的频域指纹特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究级联源-探针几何结构中，单超导量子比特上的量子波混合如何产生相干边峰层次结构，以及这些边峰如何对源量子比特发射辐射的光子统计特性敏感。

Method: 从弱驱动级联主方程出发，发展了解析理论；通过对比两种极限情况（γ_s≫γ_pr和γ_pr≫γ_s）下的边峰行为，并采用数值解验证解析标度律。

Result: 在γ_s≫γ_pr的相干滤波极限下，恢复标准相干-相干波混合层次；在γ_pr≫γ_s的相反极限下，与反聚束源场多光子吸收相关的边峰被参数化抑制。

Conclusion: 所得到的边带层次结构可作为反聚束行波微波光的频域指纹特征。

Abstract: Quantum wave mixing on a single superconducting qubit produces a hierarchy of coherent side peaks associated with elastic multiphoton scattering pathways. In a cascaded source--probe geometry these pathways become sensitive to the photon statistics of the radiation emitted by the source qubit. We develop an analytical theory of this effect starting from the cascaded master equation in the weak-driving regime. In the coherent-filtering limit $γ_{\rm s}\ggγ_{\rm pr}$, the standard coherent--coherent wave-mixing hierarchy is recovered. In the opposite limit $γ_{\rm pr}\ggγ_{\rm s}$, side peaks associated with multiphoton absorption from the antibunched source field are parametrically suppressed. Numerical solutions confirm the analytical scaling laws. The resulting sideband hierarchy provides a frequency-domain fingerprint of antibunched itinerant microwave light.

</details>


### [115] [A Variational Dissipative Framework for Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25841)
*Yuan Yao,Ruipeng Xing,Yongjian Gu,Yiming Huang,Xiao Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种变分耗散框架，将辅助比特辅助的工程耗散集成到参数化量子算法中，通过可训练的非幺正变换扩展变分量子树能制备的混合态类别，并在基态搜索和态恢复两个任务中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准变分量子电路仅由幺正操作构成，所能探索的量子态族受限。为了突破这一限制，需要引入工程耗散来扩展变分算法可制备的状态空间。

Method: 提出变分耗散框架，在参数化量子算法中集成辅助比特辅助的工程耗散。系统量子层与可训练耗散模块交替组合，通过参数化的系统-辅助比特耦合、辅助比特重置和迹操作实现非幺正变换。

Result: 在基态搜索任务中，该框架集成为耗散变分量子本征求解器，改善了向低能态的收敛；在态恢复任务中，作为恢复通道进行训练，抑制了制备噪声并提高了与目标态的保真度。

Conclusion: 工程耗散可以作为可重用的可训练原语集成到变分量子电路中，而不仅仅被视为噪声源。辅助比特辅助的耗散通道是一种具体的变分资源，能够在统一设计下同时支持优化和恢复任务。

Abstract: Dissipation engineering has attracted growing interest as an approach to controlling open quantum systems through engineered system-environment interactions. Standard variational quantum circuits are usually built from unitary operations and therefore explore only a restricted family of states. To go beyond this limitation, we introduce a variational dissipative framework in which ancilla-assisted engineered dissipation is incorporated into parameterized quantum algorithms. In this framework, system-only variational layers are combined with trainable dissipative modules, so that the circuit can prepare a broader class of mixed states through ancilla-assisted nonunitary transformations. Within this framework, the same ancilla-assisted dissipative block is used in two representative settings with different objectives. For ground-state search, it is integrated into a dissipative variational quantum eigensolver to improve the convergence toward low-energy states. For state recovery, it is trained as a recovery channel to suppress preparation noise and enhance fidelity with the target state. In both cases, the block is realized through parameterized system-ancilla couplings followed by ancilla reset and trace-out. Our results show that engineered dissipation can be incorporated into variational quantum circuits as a reusable trainable primitive rather than treated only as a source of noise. In this sense, the proposed framework identifies ancilla-assisted dissipative channels as a concrete variational resource that can support both optimization and recovery tasks within a unified design.

</details>


### [116] [Steady-state phases in long-range measurement-only quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25845)
*Bihui Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类仅包含长程测量的量子电路，发现测量范围对稳态相位有重要影响：短程测量可产生对称保护拓扑序，长程测量则导致超越面积律的纠缠标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索仅通过测量驱动的量子多体系统能否产生非平庸稳态及其动力学相变，特别是测量范围如何影响系统相位。

Method: 研究了一类包含竞争性两比特和三比特测量的长程测量-only量子电路，通过分析稳态结构、序参量、空间关联和纠缠熵来表征不同测量范围下的相行为。

Result: 短程测量可产生具有鲁棒拓扑边缘模的SPT序；中等长程测量下常规序参量被抑制但空间关联仍非平庸；足够长程测量可产生超越面积律的显著纠缠。

Conclusion: 测量范围是决定测量-only量子电路稳态相位的关键控制参数，无需幺正演化即可实现从拓扑相到大纠缠相的丰富相图。

Abstract: Measurements can drive quantum many-body systems into nontrivial steady states and induce interesting dynamical phase transitions, rendering measurement-only quantum circuits a useful platform for exploring quantum many-body phases beyond those of equilibrium Hamiltonian systems. Here we study a class of long-range measurement-only quantum circuits with competing two-qubit and three-qubit measurements. We demonstrate that these circuits exhibit rich steady-state structure and uncover a strong influence of the measurement range on the resulting phases. In particular, states with symmetry-protected topological (SPT) order can emerge with sufficiently short-range measurements beyond the nearest-neighbor limit. These states feature robust topological edge modes, which can also be detected from circuit dynamics. With longer-range measurements, an extended parameter regime emerges in which conventional order parameters are suppressed while spatial correlations remain nontrivial. Moreover, we show that in this circuit model sufficiently long-range measurements can produce significant entanglement with scaling beyond an area law despite the absence of any unitary evolution.

</details>


### [117] [Photon position eigenstates in configuration space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25857)
*Artemio González-López,Luis Martínez Alonso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了Hawton光子位置算符在配置空间中的本征函数表达式，涵盖了Riemann-Silberstein和Landau-Peierls两类波函数。这些本征函数在动量空间形式简单，但在配置空间中的表征较为复杂。作者给出了用第一类和第二类完全椭圆积分线性组合表示的闭式表达式，其模量依赖于极角的三角函数。研究表明，这些本征函数不仅在位置本征值q处发散，还在包含q的平面上发散，并且随距离q的负幂次衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了明确表征Hawton光子位置算符的本征函数在配置空间中的解析形式，填补该算符在坐标空间表示的理论空白。

Method: 通过数学推导，将动量空间中的简单本征函数形式转换到配置空间，利用第一类和第二类完全椭圆积分的线性组合给出闭式表达式。

Result: 获得了Hawton光子位置算符本征函数在配置空间中的闭式表达式，发现其在位置本征值q处和包含q的平面上均发散，且随距离呈负幂次衰减。

Conclusion: 该工作为光子位置算符的本征函数提供了完整的配置空间解析描述，揭示了其奇异性结构和衰减行为，对量子光学和光子局域化理论有重要意义。

Abstract: The expressions of the eigenfunctions of the Hawton photon position operator in the configuration space are derived for several classes of wave function, including the Riemann-Silberstein and Landau-Peierls cases. Although these eigenfunctions have a simple form in momentum space, the explicit characterization of their representations in the configuration space is rather more involved. We provide closed expressions of these eigenfunctions in terms of linear combinations of the complete elliptic integrals $K(κ)$ and $E(κ)$ with modulus $κ$ depending on trigonometric functions of the polar angle. We show that they diverge not only at the value $\mathbf q$ of the position eigenvalue, but also on a plane containing $\mathbf q$ and that they decay as inverse powers of the distance from $\mathbf q$.

</details>


### [118] [Geometric Quantization on Orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25858)
*Peiyuan Teng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在轨道流形（orbifolds）上系统发展了几何量子化理论，包括预量子化、极化和元辛修正等核心概念的新处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将几何量子化从光滑流形推广到轨道流形这一更一般的几何对象上，为具有奇点（orbifold奇异点）的辛流形提供量子化的数学框架。

Method: 在回顾轨道流形必要背景知识的基础上，分别对预量子化、极化和元辛修正这三个几何量子化的关键步骤进行了重新定义和理论构建。

Result: 成功建立了轨道流形上的几何量子化理论体系，给出了预量子化线丛、极化选择以及元辛修正在该框架下的具体形式。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典几何量子化理论推广到了更广泛的辛轨道流形范畴，为处理带有轨道奇点的物理系统提供了数学基础。

Abstract: This text introduces geometric quantization on orbifolds. After reviewing the necessary background, it develops new treatments of prequantization, polarizations, and metaplectic correction for symplectic orbifolds.

</details>


### [119] [Unified Flux Control Architecture for Fluxonium Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25948)
*Xianchuang Pan,Jiahui Wang,Tao Zhou,Yanbo Guo,Fei Wang,Ze Zhan,Liang Xiang,Zishuo Li,Lu Ma,Xizheng Ma,Huijuan Zhan,Tao Zhang,Kannan Lu,Xing Zhu,Guicheng Gong,Chunqing Deng,Tenghui Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于超导通量量子比特的统一通量控制架构，通过单一通量控制通道和单一任意波形发生器通道同时实现横向（XY）和纵向（Z）操作。通过频率选择性低温滤波和补偿波形合成，解决了共享控制通道中低频通量传输与高频噪声抑制的竞争需求。实验实现了超过100μs的相干时间、约98%的主动复位保真度、以及超过99.99%的20ns单量子比特门保真度，并展示了FPGA原生的基于可重用脉冲原语的指令级波形合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为超导量子处理器的持续扩展，需要降低硬件开销同时保持高保真操作的控制架构。现有架构中，通量量子比特的横向和纵向操作通常需要独立的控制通道，增加了硬件复杂度和布线开销。

Method: 1) 统一通量控制架构：通过单一通量控制通道实现XY和Z操作；2) 频率选择性低温滤波：在低温端抑制靠近量子比特跃迁频率的宽带噪声；3) 补偿波形合成：校正由滤波控制线引入的脉冲失真；4) FPGA原生指令级波形合成：基于可重用脉冲原语实现。

Result: 相干时间超过100μs；主动复位保真度约98%；20ns单量子比特门保真度超过99.99%。

Conclusion: 统一通量控制是一种可扩展的通量量子比特架构，在减少控制硬件开销的同时保持了高保真操作，为超导量子处理器的规模化发展提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Control architectures that reduce hardware overhead while maintaining high-fidelity operations are essential for the continued scaling of superconducting quantum processors. Here we experimentally realize a unified control architecture for fluxonium qubits, in which both transverse ($XY$) and longitudinal ($Z$) operations are implemented through a single flux-control channel driven by a single arbitrary waveform generator channel. This architecture imposes competing requirements on the shared control channel, which must simultaneously support low-frequency flux transmission for reset operations while strongly attenuating broadband noise near the qubit transition frequency. We address this challenge through frequency-selective cryogenic filtering together with compensated waveform synthesis that corrects the pulse distortion introduced by the filtered control line. Experimentally, this approach preserves coherence times above 100 $μ$s while enabling active reset with approximately 98% fidelity and 20-ns single-qubit gates with fidelities exceeding 99.99%. We further demonstrate FPGA-native instruction-level waveform synthesis based on reusable pulse primitives for unified flux control. These results establish unified flux control as a scalable architecture for fluxonium qubits that reduces control hardware overhead while preserving high-fidelity operation.

</details>


### [120] [PauLIB: A High-Performance Library for Processing Pauli Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25974)
*Florian Krötz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: PauLIB 是一个基于位压缩二元辛格表示、排序数组布局和结构体数组（SoA）内存布局的 C++20 库，在 Pauli 和运算上相比现有框架（PauliEngine、Qiskit、PennyLane）实现 10-21000 倍加速，并将内存占用减少 7.3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理大规模 Pauli 和是量子化学、Pauli 传播和基于 Pauli 的编译中的显著瓶颈。现有框架受 Python 解释器开销或哈希表数据结构影响，阻碍了 SIMD 向量化和多线程合并。

Method: 三个关键架构选择：1) 位压缩二元辛格表示（每量子位编码为2比特，Pauli乘法降为按位XOR+popcount）；2) 排序数组布局替代哈希表，实现分支可预测的SIMD批量操作；3) 结构体数组（SoA）内存布局暴露连续字数组以支持显式SIMD向量化。

Result: 500量子位基准测试：单Pauli字符串乘法25ns/操作（比PauliEngine快14倍，比Qiskit-flat快660倍）；哈密顿量外积乘法约快10倍（PauliEngine）和45倍（Qiskit）；贪心交换分组达21000倍加速（对比PennyLane）；100万项哈密顿量内存从1036MB降至142MB（7.3倍减少）。

Conclusion: PauLIB 通过紧凑位表示和高效内存布局，在Pauli运算上实现数量级的性能提升和内存节省，开源且提供C++和Python接口。

Abstract: Processing large Pauli sums is a significant bottleneck in quantum chemistry, Pauli propagation, and Pauli-based compilation. Existing frameworks often suffer from Python interpreter overhead or utilize hash-map data structures that hinder SIMD vectorization and complicate multi-threaded merging. We present PauLIB, a header-only C++20 library designed to eliminate these bottlenecks through three key architectural choices. A bit-packed binary symplectic representation that encodes each qubit in two bits, reducing Pauli multiplication to a bitwise XOR and a population count; a sorted array layout that replaces hash maps to enable branch-predictable SIMD bulk operations; and a struct-of-arrays (SoA) memory layout that exposes contiguous word arrays for explicit SIMD vectorization. Benchmarks at 500 qubits show that single Pauli string multiplication runs at 25ns per operation-14 times faster than PauliEngine and 660 times faster than Qiskit-flat across all pair counts tested. Hamiltonian outer-product multiplication is approximately 10 times faster than PauliEngine and 45 times faster than Qiskit at all tested sizes. Greedy commutation grouping, the dominant preprocessing cost in variational algorithms, achieves up to 21,000 times speedup over PennyLane, driven by the compact bit-packed representation. The compact layout reduces the memory footprint of a one-million-term Hamiltonian at 500 qubits from 1,036MB (Qiskit) to 142MB, a 7.3 times reduction that directly enables larger problem sizes within a fixed memory budget. PauLIB is open source and provides C++ and Python interfaces.

</details>


### [121] [Evaluating System-Level Fidelity with Peaked Random Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25983)
*Martin Brieger,Florian Krötz,Minh Chung,Dieter Kranzlmüller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将峰值随机电路（PRCs）重新用作系统级保真度基准，通过在不同量子比特数和电路深度下运行PRC矩阵，量化量子处理器在噪声、门误差和连接约束下识别确定性峰值的能力，并在超导和离子阱架构上验证了其作为高精度、跨平台可比性能指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算正从实验原型转向商用系统，亟需架构无关的性能指标以实现跨平台比较。现有的基准如量子体积（Quantum Volume）对干扰效应不够敏感，因此需要一种更精确、鲁棒的评估方法。

Method: 将峰值随机电路（PRCs）重新用作系统级保真度基准。通过运行包含不同量子比特数和电路深度的PRC矩阵，测量系统在累积噪声、门误差和连接约束下识别确定性峰值输出的能力，从而量化系统保真度。在IQM的超导和AQT的离子阱架构上进行实验验证。

Result: PRC基准提供了与量子体积相当的高精度度量，同时对干扰效应表现出更高的敏感性，能够更细致地区分不同硬件平台的性能差异。

Conclusion: PRCs为评估NISQ硬件的计算可靠性提供了一个鲁棒的跨平台比较框架，可作为架构无关的性能基准工具。

Abstract: Quantum computing is transitioning from experimental prototypes to commercially available turnkey systems, making architecture-agnostic performance metrics essential for cross-platform comparison. Peaked Random Circuits (PRCs) have recently been proposed as a viable path to demonstrate quantum advantage on NISQ devices: a quantum processor can reliably detect a single, peaked output state amid background noise, yet the circuits' characteristics render classical simulation infeasible. In this paper, we repurpose PRCs as a system-level fidelity benchmark. By successively running a matrix of PRCs with varying qubit counts and circuit depths, we quantify a system's ability to identify the deterministic peak despite cumulative noise, gate errors, and connectivity constraints. We apply the benchmark on IQM's superconducting and AQT's trapped-ion architectures. Our results show that PRCs provide a high-precision metric comparable to Quantum Volume while exhibiting greater sensitivity to interference effects. Consequently, PRCs enable a robust framework for assessing the computational reliability of NISQ hardware across platforms.

</details>


### [122] [Training-Free Quantum Generative Paradigm via Local Parent Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25986)
*Shu Tian,Jiaqi Hu,Rebing Wu,Yu Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的量子生成范式，通过构造局部父哈密顿量并求解全局哈密顿量，实现无参数训练的图像和文本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型需要大量计算资源、面临可扩展性限制且缺乏可解释性，现有方法如同黑箱运作。

Method: 构造一个基态编码目标分布的局部父哈密顿量，然后求解对应的全局哈密顿量，利用量子叠加和纠缠特性实现生成过程。

Result: 能够在无需参数训练的情况下实现图像和文本生成。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种基于量子力学原理（叠加与纠缠）的生成建模新路径，能够保持全局一致性。

Abstract: We propose a training-free quantum generative paradigm, which is fundamentally different from current generative models, which demand substantial computational power, face practical scalability limits, and often function as opaque black boxes, despite their remarkable success. We enable image and text generation without parameter training, by constructing a local parent Hamiltonian whose ground state encodes the target distribution and then solving the global Hamiltonian. Rooted directly in quantum mechanical principles, this approach establishes a new pathway for generative modeling that leverages superposition and entanglement to maintain global consistency.

</details>


### [123] [Toward General Quantum Control with Physics-Informed Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26021)
*Yusheng Zhao,Han Wang,Xin Liu,Xinjie Song,Jixi He,Lingwei Song,Yuanhe Ji,Ken Deng,Runqing Zhang,Zhiguo Huang,Ling Qian,Jize Han,Di Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了VF-QCTRL，一个物理信息增强的大语言模型框架，用于通用量子控制。它结合符号推理与优化，能够提出解析控制方案并通过反馈精炼参数。同时构建了包含16个任务的基准QCTRL-BENCH。实验表明该方法在普适性、准确性、效率和可解释性方面均表现优异，无需任务特定训练即可达到或超越传统最先进求解器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子控制在量子信息科技中至关重要，但由于复杂优化景观、硬件噪声和长脉冲序列，设计高保真度控制协议仍然充满挑战。现有数值求解器需要问题特定工程且产生不透明的控制振幅，而朴素的大语言模型缺乏物理一致性和长程精度，无法可靠地进行量子控制合成。

Method: 提出VF-QCTRL框架，将物理信息增强的大语言模型与符号推理及优化相结合，通过反馈机制提出解析控制方案并迭代精炼参数。同时构建QCTRL-BENCH基准，涵盖单/多量子比特系统、封闭/开放量子动力学、无噪声/有噪声设置、解析/数值协议等16个任务进行系统评估。

Result: 在基准测试中，VF-QCTRL展现出强普适性、准确性、效率和可解释性：无需任务特定训练即可应用于通用量子控制系统，在无噪声和噪声环境下性能均达到或超越传统最先进求解器，具有有利的推理时缩放和脉冲分辨率缩放特性，并能从提示中直接推导出物理可解释的解析协议。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了基于物理信息增强的大语言模型的量子控制作为一种有前景的新范式，能够实现准确、高效、可解释且无需训练的量子控制协议设计，适用于广泛的量子系统。

Abstract: Quantum control is essential for quantum information science and technology, yet designing high-fidelity control protocols remains challenging due to complex optimization landscapes, hardware noise, and long pulse sequences. Existing numerical solvers often require problem-specific engineering and produce opaque control amplitudes, while naive large language models (LLMs) lack the physical consistency and long-horizon precision for reliable quantum control synthesis. Here we introduce VF-QCTRL, a physics-informed large language model framework for general quantum control that combines symbolic reasoning with optimization to propose analytic control ansätze and coherently refine their parameters through feedback. To systematically evaluate LLM-driven quantum control, we develop QCTRL-BENCH, a benchmark spanning sixteen tasks across single- and multi-qubit systems, closed and open quantum dynamics, noiseless and noisy settings, and both analytic and numerical protocols. Across the benchmark, VF-QCTRL demonstrates strong universality, accuracy, efficiency, and interpretability: it applies to generic quantum control systems without task-specific training, achieves performance competitive with or exceeding state-of-the-art conventional solvers in both noiseless and noisy regimes with query efficiency, exhibits favorable inference-time scaling and pulse resolution scaling, and derives physically interpretable analytical protocols directly from prompts. Our results establish physics-informed LLM-based quantum control as a promising paradigm for accurate, efficient, interpretable, and training-free quantum control protocol design across a broad range of quantum systems.

</details>


### [124] [Asymptotically Optimal Depth Fermionic Permutation on 2D Grid Quantum Architecture without Ancillas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26041)
*Dantong Li,Shifan Xu,Yongshan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对二维网格架构的费米子排列协议，实现了最优的O(√N)深度和O(N√N)最近邻门操作，无需辅助量子比特、中间测量或经典前馈。该协议还构建了Jordan-Wigner、Bravyi-Kitaev和Parity编码之间的O(√N)深度变换，并在费米子快速傅里叶变换和稀疏SYK模型的Trotter模拟中验证了其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子硬件上模拟费米子系统涉及大量非局域相互作用，高效路由这些相互作用对费米子模拟算法的整体成本至关重要。现有方法在全连接架构下实现了多对数深度，但在二维最近邻架构下退化为O(√N polylog N)。本文旨在为二维网格架构设计最优的费米子路由方案。

Method: 提出一种费米子排列协议，利用希尔伯特曲线布局在二维网格上实现最优O(√N)深度，无需辅助量子比特、中间测量或经典前馈。同时构建了Jordan-Wigner、Bravyi-Kitaev和Parity三种编码之间的高效变换。

Result: 实现了最优O(√N)深度和O(N√N)最近邻门操作，匹配Ω(√N)下界（即使允许O(N)辅助量子比特和经典前馈）。在费米子快速傅里叶变换和稀疏SYK模型的Trotter模拟基准测试中，对于N≳100的系统，在早期容错量子计算场景下一致降低了深度、时空体积和保真度损失。

Conclusion: 本文提出的协议为二维网格架构上的费米子模拟提供了最优的深度路由方案，适用于所有三种主要费米子编码，并在实际基准测试中展示了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Simulating fermionic systems on qubit hardware involves many nonlocal interactions, and efficient routing of these interactions is critical to the overall cost of fermionic simulation algorithms. Recent works reduce this Jordan-Wigner routing overhead to polylogarithmic depth under all-to-all connectivity, but degrade to $O(\sqrt{N}\,\mathrm{polylog}\,N)$ for $N$ fermionic modes on 2D nearest-neighbor architectures. We present a fermionic permutation protocol tailored to 2D grid architectures that achieves the optimal $O(\sqrt{N})$ depth with $O(N\sqrt{N})$ nearest-neighbor gates and no ancilla qubits, mid-circuit measurements, or classical feedforward. This matches the $Ω(\sqrt{N})$ lower bound, which holds even when $O(N)$ ancillas and classical feedforward are permitted. We further construct an $O(\sqrt{N})$-depth transformation between the Jordan-Wigner, Bravyi-Kitaev, and Parity encodings on the 2D grid via a Hilbert-curve layout, extending our result to all three encodings. Benchmarks on the fermionic fast Fourier transform and Trotter simulation of sparse SYK model demonstrate consistent reduction in depth, spacetime volume, and infidelity for system sizes $N \gtrsim 100$ in the early fault-tolerant regime.

</details>


### [125] [Rounding Almost Commuting Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26096)
*Islam Faisal,Anand Natarajan,Alexander Poremba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种保持局域性的算法舍入技术，能够将任意几乎对易的2-局域量子比特哈密顿量近似为对易的哈密顿量，误差界为O(mε^{1/6})，并由此证明了在特定条件下基态能量的δ-近似属于NP问题，同时展示了该框架在Gibbs采样和快速哈密顿模拟中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理哈密顿量极少严格对易，因此研究近似对易（almost commuting）的哈密顿量具有自然动机。尽管几乎对易哈密顿量在量子多体物理与经典约束满足问题之间架起桥梁，但近似对易的 implications 尚未被充分理解。

Method: 设计了一种保持局域性的算法舍入技术（locality-preserving algorithmic rounding），将任意2-局域量子比特哈密顿量H=∑h_i（满足‖[h_i,h_j]‖≤ε）映射到邻近的对易哈密顿量Ĥ，并保证整体距离‖H-Ĥ‖=O(mε^{1/6})。

Result: 当δ≫mε^{1/6}时，ε-几乎对易的2-局域量子比特哈密顿量的基态能量δ-近似属于NP；此外，该框架可应用于近乎对易系统的Gibbs采样和快速哈密顿模拟。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的对易哈密顿量可解性推广到了近似对易情形，为理解近似对易哈密顿量的计算复杂性提供了新途径，并展示了实用的量子计算应用。

Abstract: Commuting Hamiltonians lie at the boundary between classical constraint satisfaction and quantum many-body physics, exhibiting rich quantum structure while remaining more tractable than general noncommuting models. In contrast, physical Hamiltonians are rarely exactly commuting, which naturally motivates the study of almost commuting Hamiltonians. Despite their relevance, the implications of approximate commutation are only poorly understood.
  In this work, we show how to efficiently approximate any almost commuting $2$-local qubit Hamiltonian by a commuting one: we give a locality-preserving algorithmic rounding technique that maps any $2$-local Hamiltonian $H=\sum_{i=1}^m h_i$ with $\|[h_i,h_j]\| \leq ε$ to a nearby Hamiltonian $\hat{H}$ whose terms pair-wise commute, and which is within overall distance $\|H-\hat{H}\| = O(m\,ε^{1/6})$. As a consequence, we show that $δ$-approximations to the ground energy for $ε$-almost commuting $2$-local qubit Hamiltonians lie in $\mathsf{NP}$ when $δ\gg mε^{1/6}$, extending the classical containment well beyond the commuting setting. Finally, we present two applications of our rounding framework: Gibbs sampling and fast Hamiltonian simulation for almost commuting systems.

</details>
